[];\\r\\n\\r\\n  // \\"Read, Recall, Colour\\" matches the door and all 12 sections. Prefer the\\r\\n  // exact title, which is the door — the surface that lets her pick a section.\\r\\n  const exact = rows.filter((r) => r.title.toLowerCase() === q.toLowerCase());\\r\\n  const pool = exact.length ? exact : rows;\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (pool.length === 0) {\\r\\n    console.error(`assign: no Activity matches \\"${query}\\".`);\\r\\n    process.exit(1);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  if (pool.length > 1) {\\r\\n    console.error(`assign: \\"${query}\\" matches ${pool.length} Activities:`);\\r\\n    for (const r of pool) console.error(`  - ${r.title}  (${r.slug})`);\\r\\n    process.exit(1);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return pool[0];\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function resolveDrill(query: string) {\\r\\n  const q = query.trim();\\r\\n  const bySlug = await supabase.from(\\"guided_drills\\").select(\\"id,title,slug\\").eq(\\"slug\\", q).maybeSingle();\\r\\n  if (bySlug.data) return bySlug.data;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\\"guided_drills\\")\\r\\n    .select(\\"id,title,slug\\")\\r\\n    .ilike(\\"title\\", `%${q}%`);\\r\\n  if (error) throw error;\\r\\n  const rows = data ?? [];\\r\\n  if (rows.length === 0) {\\r\\n    console.error(`assign: no guided drill matches \\"${query}\\".`);\\r\\n    process.exit(1);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  if (rows.length > 1) {\\r\\n    console.error(`assign: \\"${query}\\" matches ${rows.length} drills:`);\\r\\n    for (const r of rows) console.error(`  - ${r.title}  (${r.slug})`);\\r\\n    process.exit(1);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return rows[0];\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n// ------------------------------------------------------- PT sections / sets\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * A timed PT section is a homework set whose qids are exactly one section of\\r\\n * one PrepTest. `drillRunSpec.singleSectionOf()` notices that shape and runs\\r\\n * it as a real 35-minute `full-section` instead of an untimed drill, so the\\r\\n * qids have to come from the section file itself, in order, with nothing\\r\\n * else mixed in. The file under public/data is the same one the section\\r\\n * runner loads, so the set can never name a question the runner lacks.\\r\\n */\\r\\nconst SECTION_PATTERN = /^PT\\\\s*(\\\\d+)[.\\\\-\\\\s]*S(?:ection)?\\\\s*(\\\\d+)$/i;\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction sectionTitle(pt: number, section: number) {\\r\\n  return `PT${pt}.S${section} (LR)`;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction readSectionQids(query: string): { pt: number; section: number; qids: string[] } {\\r\\n  const m = SECTION_PATTERN.exec(query.trim());\\r\\n  if (!m) {\\r\\n    console.error(`assign: --section wants PT<n>.S<n> (e.g. PT156.S2), got \\"${query}\\".`);\\r\\n    process.exit(2);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  const pt = Number(m[1]);\\r\\n  const section = Number(m[2]);\\r\\n  const dataDir = path.join(REPO, \\"public\\", \\"data\\");\\r\\n  const file = path.join(dataDir, `PT${pt}-S${section}-LR.json`);\\r\\n  if (!fs.existsSync(file)) {\\r\\n    const have = fs\\r\\n      .readdirSync(dataDir)\\r\\n      .filter((f) => f.startsWith(`PT${pt}-`) && f.endsWith(\\"-LR.json\\"))\\r\\n      .map((f) => f.replace(/-LR\\\\.json$/, \\"\\").replace(\\"-\\", \\".\\"));\\r\\n    console.error(\\r\\n      `assign: no LR section file for PT${pt}.S${section}.` +\\r\\n        (have.length ? ` PT${pt} has: ${have.join(\\", \\")}.` : ` Nothing for PT${pt} is loaded.`),\\r\\n    );\\r\\n    process.exit(1);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  const rows = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(file, \\"utf8\\")) as Array<{\\r\\n    practiceTest: string;\\r\\n    sectionNumber: number;\\r\\n    questionNumber: number;\\r\\n  }>;\\r\\n  const qids = rows\\r\\n    .filter((r) => r.practiceTest === `PT${pt}` && r.sectionNumber === section)\\r\\n    .sort((a, b) => a.questionNumber - b.questionNumber)\\r\\n    .map((r) => `PT${pt}-S${section}-Q${r.questionNumber}`);\\r\\n  if (qids.length < 20) {\\r\\n    console.error(`assign: ${file} holds ${qids.length} questions; a full LR section has 24-26.`);\\r\\n    process.exit(1);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return { pt, section, qids };\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * One set per section, reused. Two sets with the same title would make the\\r\\n * teacher UI show duplicates and the unique index on (student, set) useless.\\r\\n */\\r\\nasync function findOrCreateSectionSet(teacherId: string, query: string, write: boolean) {\\r\\n  const { pt, section, qids } = readSectionQids(query);\\r\\n  const title = sectionTitle(pt, section);\\r\\n  const { data: existing, error } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\\"homework_sets\\")\\r\\n    .select(\\"id,title,question_qids,estimated_minutes\\")\\r\\n    .eq(\\"title\\", title)\\r\\n    .order(\\"created_at\\", { ascending: true });\\r\\n  if (error) throw error;\\r\\n  const match = (existing ?? []).find(\\r\\n    (s) => JSON.stringify(s.question_qids ?? []) === JSON.stringify(qids),\\r\\n  );\\r\\n  if (match) {\\r\\n    // The classroom card reads this; without it she is told \\"about 50\\r\\n    // minutes\\" for a 35-minute section.\\r\\n    if (match.estimated_minutes !== 35 && write) {\\r\\n      await supabase.from(\\"homework_sets\\").update({ estimated_minutes: 35 }).eq(\\"id\\", match.id);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    return { id: match.id, title: match.title, created: false, count: qids.length };\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  if (existing?.length) {\\r\\n    console.error(\\r\\n      `assign: a set titled \\"${title}\\" exists but its questions differ from PT${pt}-S${section}-LR.json.` +\\r\\n        \\" Fix the set in Teacher > Homework before assigning.\\",\\r\\n    );\\r\\n    process.exit(1);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  if (!write) return { id: \\"(new)\\", title, created: true, count: qids.length };\\r\\n  const { data, error: insErr } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\\"homework_sets\\")\\r\\n    .insert({\\r\\n      title,\\r\\n      description: `PrepTest ${pt}, Section ${section} - Logical Reasoning. Standard time.`,\\r\\n      question_qids: qids,\\r\\n      created_by: teacherId,\\r\\n      class_id: teacherId,\\r\\n      estimated_minutes: 35,\\r\\n    })\\r\\n    .select(\\"id,title\\")\\r\\n    .single();\\r\\n  if (insErr) throw insErr;\\r\\n  return { id: data.id, title: data.title, created: true, count: qids.length };\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function resolveSet(query: string) {\\r\\n  const q = query.trim();\\r\\n  if (/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/i.test(q)) {\\r\\n    const { data } = await supabase.from(\\"homework_sets\\").select(\\"id,title\\").eq(\\"id\\", q).maybeSingle();\\r\\n    if (data) return data;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.from(\\"homework_sets\\").select(\\"id,title\\").ilike(\\"title\\", `%${q}%`);\\r\\n  if (error) throw error;\\r\\n  const rows = data ?? [];\\r\\n  const exact = rows.filter((r) => r.title.toLowerCase() === q.toLowerCase());\\r\\n  const pool = exact.length ? exact : rows;\\r\\n  if (pool.length === 0) {\\r\\n    console.error(`assign: no homework set matches \\"${query}\\".`);\\r\\n    process.exit(1);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  if (pool.length > 1) {\\r\\n    console.error(`assign: \\"${query}\\" matches ${pool.length} sets:`);\\r\\n    for (const r of pool) console.error(`  - ${r.title}  (${r.id})`);\\r\\n    process.exit(1);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return pool[0];\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Due at the end of that calendar day, local time: \\"due Saturday\\" means all of Saturday. */\\r\\nfunction dueAtEndOfDay(day: string): string {\\r\\n  const [y, m, d] = day.split(\\"-\\").map(Number);\\r\\n  return new Date(y, m - 1, d, 23, 59, 59).toISOString();\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------- messaging\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Mirrors findOrCreateThread in src/lib/classroomNotify.ts, which is private. */\\r\\nasync function findOrCreateThread(adminUserId: string, studentId: string): Promise<string> {\\r\\n  const { data: mine } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\\"conversation_participants\\")\\r\\n    .select(\\"conversation_id\\")\\r\\n    .eq(\\"user_id\\", adminUserId);\\r\\n  const { data: theirs } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\\"conversation_participants\\")\\r\\n    .select(\\"conversation_id\\")\\r\\n    .eq(\\"user_id\\", studentId);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const ids = new Set((mine ?? []).map((r) => r.conversation_id));\\r\\n  const shared = (theirs ?? []).find((r) => ids.has(r.conversation_id));\\r\\n  if (shared) return shared.conversation_id as string;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const { data: conv, error } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\\"conversations\\")\\r\\n    .insert({})\\r\\n    .select(\\"id\\")\\r\\n    .single();\\r\\n  if (error || !conv) throw error ?? new Error(\\"could not create conversation\\");\\r\\n\\r\\n  const { error: pErr } = await supabase.from(\\"conversation_participants\\").insert([\\r\\n    { conversation_id: conv.id, user_id: adminUserId },\\r\\n    { conversation_id: conv.id, user_id: studentId },\\r\\n  ]);\\r\\n  if (pErr) throw pErr;\\r\\n  return conv.id as string;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n// --------------------------------------------------------------------- main\\r\\n\\r\\n// ------------------------------------------------------------ access\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * The flag that has to be on for a student to open what she was just assigned.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Every route into this work sits behind a `ProtectedRoute`, and Classroom\'s\\r\\n * own feed drops rows a student cannot open, so a missing flag does not read as\\r\\n * a locked door: the work is simply invisible, and the link Joshua sends says\\r\\n * \\"Not open yet\\".\\r\\n *\\r\\n * That happened. Ivory Johnson was assigned three Read, Recall, Colour sections\\r\\n * on 2026-09-08 with `has_activity_access` off. He mailed Joshua on the Monday\\r\\n * to say the portal was empty, tried the link on the Tuesday, was told it was\\r\\n * not in his plan, and lost a day of study to a boolean. Nothing in the assign\\r\\n * path had any opinion about whether he could reach what it was writing.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Assigning work he cannot open is never what Joshua means, so this grants the\\r\\n * one flag the assigned work needs. It never grants anything else and it never\\r\\n * revokes: the rest of the feature set stays exactly where he set it.\\r\\n */\\r\\nconst ACCESS_FLAGS = {\\r\\n  activity: [\\"has_activity_access\\"],\\r\\n  guided_drill: [\\"has_drill_access\\"],\\r\\n  // A homework set is opened from /classroom/:id and run at /drill.\\r\\n  homework_set: [\\"has_classroom_access\\", \\"has_drill_access\\"],\\r\\n} as const;\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function ensureCanOpen(classId: string, kind: keyof typeof ACCESS_FLAGS, who: string) {\\r\\n  for (const flag of ACCESS_FLAGS[kind]) await ensureFlag(classId, flag, who);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function ensureFlag(classId: string, flag: string, who: string) {\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\\"profiles\\")\\r\\n    .select(flag)\\r\\n    .eq(\\"class_id\\", classId)\\r\\n    .single();\\r\\n  if (error) {\\r\\n    console.warn(`! Could not check ${flag} for ${who}: ${error.message}`);\\r\\n    console.warn(\\"  The work is assigned. Confirm they can open it before telling them it is there.\\");\\r\\n    return;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  if ((data as Record<string, boolean>)?.[flag]) return;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const { error: upErr } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\\"profiles\\")\\r\\n    .update({ [flag]: true })\\r\\n    .eq(\\"class_id\\", classId);\\r\\n  if (upErr) {\\r\\n    console.error(`! ${who} does not have ${flag} and it could not be turned on: ${upErr.message}`);\\r\\n    console.error(\\"  They will see \\\\\\"Not open yet\\\\\\". Turn it on in Admin before telling them.\\");\\r\\n    return;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  console.log(`  ${flag} was off for ${who}. Turned it on, or they could not have opened this.`);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function main() {\\r\\n  const teacherId = await signIn();\\r\\n  const student = resolveStudent(await listStudents(), studentQuery!);\\r\\n  const who = `${student.display_name ?? \\"(no name)\\"} <${student.email ?? \\"?\\"}>`;\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (messageText) {\\r\\n    console.log(`Message → ${who}\\\\n  \\"${messageText}\\"`);\\r\\n    if (dryRun) return console.log(\\"\\\\n(dry run — nothing sent)\\");\\r\\n    const conversationId = await findOrCreateThread(teacherId, student.class_id);\\r\\n    const { error } = await supabase\\r\\n      .from(\\"messages\\")\\r\\n      .insert({ conversation_id: conversationId, sender_id: teacherId, body: messageText });\\r\\n    if (error) throw error;\\r\\n    return console.log(\\"Sent.\\");\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  let item: Parameters<typeof assignWork>[0][\\"item\\"];\\r\\n  let label: string;\\r\\n  let note = \\"\\";\\r\\n  if (activityQuery) {\\r\\n    const a = await resolveActivity(activityQuery);\\r\\n    item = { kind: \\"activity\\", activityId: a.id };\\r\\n    label = a.title;\\r\\n  } else if (drillQuery) {\\r\\n    const d = await resolveDrill(drillQuery);\\r\\n    item = { kind: \\"guided_drill\\", drillId: d.id };\\r\\n    label = d.title;\\r\\n  } else if (sectionQuery) {\\r\\n    const s = await findOrCreateSectionSet(teacherId, sectionQuery, !dryRun);\\r\\n    item = { kind: \\"homework_set\\", setId: s.id };\\r\\n    label = s.title;\\r\\n    note = `  ${s.count} questions, timed 35:00 · set ${s.created ? \\"created\\" : \\"reused\\"} (${s.id})`;\\r\\n  } else {\\r\\n    const s = await resolveSet(setQuery!);\\r\\n    item = { kind: \\"homework_set\\", setId: s.id };\\r\\n    label = s.title;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (item.kind === \\"homework_set\\") {\\r\\n    // The unique index refuses a second active copy; say so in plain words\\r\\n    // rather than surfacing a Postgres constraint name.\\r\\n    const { data: prior } = await supabase\\r\\n      .from(\\"homework_assignments\\")\\r\\n      .select(\\"id,status,created_at\\")\\r\\n      .eq(\\"student_id\\", student.class_id)\\r\\n      .eq(\\"set_id\\", item.setId)\\r\\n      .neq(\\"status\\", \\"completed\\")\\r\\n      .maybeSingle();\\r\\n    if (prior) {\\r\\n      console.error(\\r\\n        `assign: ${who} already has \\"${label}\\" (${prior.status}, assigned ${new Date(prior.created_at).toLocaleDateString(\\"en-CA\\")}).` +\\r\\n          `\\r\\n  /classroom/${prior.id}. A set can be re-issued only after that copy is completed.`,\\r\\n      );\\r\\n      process.exit(1);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  console.log(\\r\\n    `Assign \\"${label}\\" → ${who}` +\\r\\n      `${scheduleFor ? `  (unlocks ${scheduleFor})` : \\"\\"}${dueOn ? `  (due ${dueOn})` : \\"\\"}`,\\r\\n  );\\r\\n  if (note) console.log(note);\\r\\n  if (dryRun) return console.log(\\"\\\\n(dry run — nothing written)\\");\\r\\n\\r\\n  const { ok, failed } = await assignWork({\\r\\n    teacherId,\\r\\n    studentIds: [student.class_id],\\r\\n    item,\\r\\n    scheduledForLocalDate: scheduleFor,\\r\\n    overrides: dueOn ? { dueAt: dueAtEndOfDay(dueOn) } : undefined,\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (failed.length) {\\r\\n    console.error(`FAILED: ${failed[0].error}`);\\r\\n    process.exit(1);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  const r = ok[0];\\r\\n  const n = r.notify;\\r\\n  // After the write, not before: a student who is never assigned anything has\\r\\n  // no business having a feature opened for them.\\r\\n  await ensureCanOpen(student.class_id, item.kind, who);\\r\\n  console.log(\\r\\n    `Assigned. id=${r.assignmentId}\\\\n` +\\r\\n      `  inbox ${n?.inbox_ok ? \\"ok\\" : \\"FAILED\\"} · bell ${n?.bell_ok ? \\"ok\\" : \\"FAILED\\"} · email ${n?.email_ok ? \\"ok\\" : \\"FAILED\\"}`,\\r\\n  );\\r\\n  if (n && !(n.inbox_ok && n.bell_ok && n.email_ok)) {\\r\\n    console.warn(\\"! The work IS assigned and visible; only a notification channel failed.\\");\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nmain()\\r\\n  .then(() => process.exit(0))\\r\\n  .catch((e) => {\\r\\n    console.error(e instanceof Error ? e.message : e);\\r\\n    process.exit(1);\\r\\n  });\\r\\n"}}\n[{"name":"mcp__lsat_u_lessons__list_student_classroom_items","description":"Read the current Classroom items and IDs for one student.\\n\\nexec tool declaration:\\n```ts\\ndeclare const tools: { mcp__lsat_u_lessons__list_student_class"},{"name":"mcp__lsat_u_lessons__publish_interactive_lesson","description":"Assign a complete HTML lesson directly to one student. Verifies byte-for-byte content, visibility, and one active copy; replaces only prior versions o"}]'}]

Use the Node CLI for other supported actions.\\n.agents/skills/lsat-u-platform-actions\\\\SKILL.md:15:For an interactive HTML lesson, call the `lsat-u-lessons` MCP tool `publish_interactive_lesson` with the student\'s exact email, absolute HTML path, and title. The local stdio server is `system/scripts/lsatu-lesson-mcp.mjs`; Codex registration is `lsat-u-lessons`. It calls `system/scripts/lsatu-admin.mjs post-lesson`, which authenticates with the existing admin credentials, posts through `mcp_post_classroom_note`, verifies the saved HTML exactly and student visibility, and then archives only earlier versions with the same lesson marker. A repeat of the current version returns the same item without another notification. Use `dry_run: true` when the student or replacement is uncertain; otherwise publish in one call. MCP tools newly registered during a session may only appear in a new Codex session; the direct Node command is the immediate fallback.\\n.codex\\\\agents\\\\plugin-dev-plugin-validator.toml:5:developer_instructions = \\"You are an expert plugin validator specializing in comprehensive validation of Codex plugin structure, configuration, and components.\\\\n\\\\n**Your Core Responsibilities:**\\\\n1. Validate plugin structure and organization\\\\n2. Check plugin.json manifest for correctness\\\\n3. Validate all component files (commands, agents, skills, hooks)\\\\n4. Verify naming conventions and file organization\\\\n5. Check for common issues and anti-patterns\\\\n6. Provide specific, actionable recommendations\\\\n\\\\n**Validation Process:**\\\\n\\\\n1. **Locate Plugin Root**:\\\\n   - Check for `.claude-plugin/plugin.json`\\\\n   - Verify plugin directory structure\\\\n   - Note plugin location (project vs marketplace)\\\\n\\\\n2. **Validate Manifest** (`.claude-plugin/plugin.json`):\\\\n   - Check JSON syntax (use Bash with `jq` or Read + manual parsing)\\\\n   - Verify required field: `name`\\\\n   - Check name format (kebab-case, no spaces)\\\\n   - Validate optional fields if present:\\\\n     - `version`: Semantic versioning format (X.Y.Z)\\\\n     - `description`: Non-empty string\\\\n     - `author`: Valid structure\\\\n     - `mcpServers`: Valid server configurations\\\\n   - Check for unknown fields (warn but don\'t fail)\\\\n\\\\n3. **Validate Directory Structure**:\\\\n   - Use Glob to find component directories\\\\n   - Check standard locations:\\\\n     - `commands/` for slash commands\\\\n     - `agents/` for agent definitions\\\\n     - `skills/` for skill directories\\\\n     - `hooks/hooks.json` for hooks\\\\n   - Verify auto-discovery works\\\\n\\\\n4. **Validate Commands** (if `commands/` exists):\\\\n   - Use Glob to find `commands/**/*.md`\\\\n   - For each command file:\\\\n     - Check YAML frontmatter present (starts with `---`)\\\\n     - Verify `description` field exists\\\\n     - Check `argument-hint` format if present\\\\n     - Validate `allowed-tools` is array if present\\\\n     - Ensure markdown content exists\\\\n   - Check for naming conflicts\\\\n\\\\n5. **Validate Agents** (if `agents/` exists):\\\\n   - Use Glob to find `agents/**/*.md`\\\\n   - For each agent file:\\\\n     - Use the validate-agent.sh utility from agent-development skill\\\\n     - Or manually check:\\\\n       - Frontmatter with `name`, `description`, `model`, `color`\\\\n       - Name format (lowercase, hyphens, 3-50 chars)\\\\n       - Description includes `<example>` blocks\\\\n       - Model is valid (inherit/sonnet/opus/haiku)\\\\n       - Color is valid (blue/cyan/green/yellow/magenta/red)\\\\n       - System prompt exists and is substantial (>20 chars)\\\\n\\\\n6. **Validate Skills** (if `skills/` exists):\\\\n   - Use Glob to find `skills/*/SKILL.md`\\\\n   - For each skill directory:\\\\n     - Verify `SKILL.md` file exists\\\\n     - Means the migration has not been applied to this database yet.\\r\\n */\\r\\nfunction isMissingColumnError(error: unknown): boolean {\\r\\n  const e = error as { code?: string; message?: string } | null;\\r\\n  if (!e) return false;\\r\\n  if (e.code === \'42703\') return true;\\r\\n  return /proficiency_level/.test(e.message ?? \'\') && /does not exist|column/i.test(e.message ?? \'\');\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Every student\'s band in one query.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Seeded with null for each requested id, so a student with no profile row is a\\r\\n * defined \\"no level\\" rather than an undefined lookup. A student Joshua has not\\r\\n * levelled gets null and therefore the drill\'s own range -- the behaviour that\\r\\n * predates levels. Inventing a default here would be the system overruling him.\\r\\n */\\r\\nasync function fetchStudentLevels(studentIds: string[]): Promise<StudentLevelMap> {\\r\\n  const levels = new Map<string, StudentLevel | null>(\\r\\n    studentIds.map((id) => [id, null]),\\r\\n  );\\r\\n  if (studentIds.length === 0) return levels;\\r\\n\\r\\n  // proficiency_level is newer than the auto-generated Supabase types, which\\r\\n  // are not hand-editable, so the select is cast at the call site -- the same\\r\\n  // pattern savedSets.ts and questionExposure.ts use for new columns.\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await (supabase.from(\'profiles\') as never as {\\r\\n    select: (cols: string) => {\\r\\n      in: (\\r\\n        col: string,\\r\\n        vals: string[],\\r\\n      ) => Promise<{ data: Array<Record<string, unknown>> | null; error: unknown }>;\\r\\n    };\\r\\n  })\\r\\n    .select(\'class_id, proficiency_level\')\\r\\n    .in(\'class_id\', studentIds);\\r\\n\\r\\n  // The code and the migration do not land atomically. If the app is live\\r\\n  // before the column exists, treat it as \\"nobody has a level yet\\" and assign\\r\\n  // exactly as we did before levels existed, rather than breaking every guided\\r\\n  // drill assign until the migration catches up. Any other error is real and\\r\\n  // fails the assign cleanly, because silently unlevelling work Joshua asked to\\r\\n  // be levelled would be worse than not assigning it.\\r\\n  if (error) {\\r\\n    if (isMissingColumnError(error)) {\\r\\n      console.warn(\'[assignWork] profiles.proficiency_level is not deployed yet; assigning unlevelled\');\\r\\n      return levels;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    throw error;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  for (const row of data ?? []) {\\r\\n    const id = row.class_id as string;\\r\\n    if (!levels.has(id)) continue;\\r\\n    levels.set(id, coerceStudentLevel(row.proficiency_level, null));\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return levels;\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"b5e93a","wall_time_seconds":1.544594,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":847,"output":"        dueAt: null,\\r\\n        metadata: { set_id: set.id },\\r\\n      };\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    case \'guided_drill\': {\\r\\n      const { data: drill, error } = await supabase\\r\\n        .from(\'guided_drills\')\\r\\n        .select(\'id, title, question_count\')\\r\\n        .eq(\'id\', item.drillId)\\r\\n        .single();\\r\\n      if (error) throw error;\\r\\n\\r\\n      return {\\r\\n        type: \'drill\',\\r\\n        title: drill.title,\\r\\n        description: `New guided drill: ${drill.title}`,\\r\\n        assignmentType: \'guided drill\',\\r\\n        sourceTable: \'guided_drill_assignments\',\\r\\n        insertRow: async (studentId) => {\\r\\n          // Normally the level is recorded, not resolved: the question draw\\r\\n          // happens when the student opens the drill, so a re-attempt gets a\\r\\n          // fresh set. That is the whole point of the pool model.\\r\\n          //\\r\\n          // Freezing is correct in exactly one case — an offline artifact was\\r\\n          // generated from a specific list and emailed. Unlocks the work that morning. */\\r\\n  scheduledForLocalDate?: string;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Teacher-authored overrides for what the student sees. The assign builder\\r\\n   * lets Joshua retitle a set or set a due date for one student without\\r\\n   * editing the set itself.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  overrides?: {\\r\\n    title?: string;\\r\\n    description?: string;\\r\\n    dueAt?: string | null;\\r\\n    metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;\\r\\n  };\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Per-student band, overriding their profile level. An explicit null assigns\\r\\n   * that student unlevelled. Guided drills only.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  levelOverrides?: Record<string, StudentLevel | null>;\\r\\n  /** Shorten the drill for everyone in this assign. Guided drills only. */\\r\\n  frozenQidsByStudent?: Record<string, string[]>;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Send the student a notification. Default true. Set false only when the\\r\\n   * caller sends its own message covering this work.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  notify?: boolean;\\r\\n}): Promise<AssignWorkResult> {\\r\\n  return runAssign({ ...input, items: [input.item] });\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Assign several pieces of work — of mixed kinds — as ONE event (spec D4).\\r\\n *\\r\\n * A drill and a homework set for the same topic ship together: the student gets\\r\\n * a classroom card for each (they are separate work), but a single notification,\\r\\n * because two pings for one handoff reads as two assignments. Per student the\\r\\n * bundle is all-or-nothing: if the third item fails, the first two are rolled\\r\\n * back, so the teacher never has to reason about a half-delivered bundle.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Per-item overrides are deliberately not offered here — retitling one item\\r\\n * inside a bundle belongs to the single-item path.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport async function assignWorkBundle(input: {\\r\\n  teacherId: string;\\r\\n  studentIds: string[];\\r\\n  items: AssignableItem[];\\r\\n  scheduledForLocalDate?: string;\\r\\n  /** Per-student band. [{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 3.4 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"bade78","wall_time_seconds":1.5282769,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":797,"output":"/**\\r\\n * The proficiency band a student works at, and how it maps onto the question\\r\\n * bank\'s 1-5 difficulty scale.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * The bands overlap on purpose. Hard cutoffs (1-2 / 3 / 4-5) starve the middle\\r\\n * band: a drill narrowed to a single difficulty value runs out of questions on\\r\\n * any question type that is not one of the big four. Overlapping bands keep\\r\\n * every level drawing from a pool large enough to fill a full drill.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * This module deliberately has no imports. The bands are needed by the assign\\r\\n * path, the runtime resolver, the teacher UI and the tests, and anything that\\r\\n * imported the supabase client or the question bank singleton to reach them\\r\\n * would drag both into every one of those callers.\\r\\n */\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type StudentLevel = \'beginner\' | \'intermediate\' | \'advanced\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport const STUDENT_LEVELS = [\'beginner\', \'intermediate\', \'advanced\'] as const;\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface DifficultyBand {\\r\\n  min: number;\\r\\n  max: number;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * The one place the band numbers are defined in TypeScript. They appear in SQL\\r\\n * exactly once more, in the guided_drills.level backfill, and a spec asserts\\r\\n * that migration still agrees with this table.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport const LEVEL_BANDS: Record<StudentLevel, DifficultyBand> = {\\r\\n  beginner: { min: 1, max: 3 },\\r\\n  intermediate: { min: 2, max: 4 },\\r\\n  advanced: { min: 3, max: 5 },\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\nexport const LEVEL_LABELS: Record<StudentLevel, string> = {\\r\\n  beginner: \'Beginner\',\\r\\n  intermediate: \'Intermediate\',\\r\\n  advanced: \'Advanced\',\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\n/** The full difficulty range, used when no level applies. */\\r\\nexport const FULL_DIFFICULTY_RANGE: DifficultyBand = { min: 1, max: 5 };\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function isStudentLevel(value: unknown): value is StudentLevel {\\r\\n  return typeof value === \'string\' && (STUDENT_LEVELS as readonly string[]).includes(value);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Narrow an untrusted value (a DB column, a URL param) to a level.\\r\\n * Strict: \'Beginner\' and \'BEGINNER\' are not levels. The stored values are\\r\\n * lowercase by CHECK constraint, so anything else is a bug worth surfacing\\r\\n * rather than silently coercing.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function coerceStudentLevel<T>(value: unknown, fallback: T): StudentLevel | T {\\r\\n  return isStudentLevel(value) ? value : fallback;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function levelBand(level: StudentLevel): DifficultyBand {\\r\\n  return LEVEL_BANDS[level];\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Midpoint of a band, used to rank out-of-band questions by nearness. */\\r\\nexport function bandMidpoint(band: DifficultyBand): number {\\r\\n  return (band.min + band.max) / 2;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function isInBand(difficulty: unknown, band: DifficultyBand): boolean {\\r\\n  // A non-finite difficulty is never in band. One question in the bank has a\\r\\n  // null difficulty, and the raw numeric comparisons used elsewhere let it pass\\r\\n  // every filter (null < 1 and null > 5 are both false).\\r\\n  if (typeof difficulty !== \'number\' || !Number.isFinite(difficulty)) return false;\\r\\n  return difficulty >= band.min && difficulty <= band.max;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Human-readable band, e.g. \\"difficulty 1-3\\". */\\r\\nexport function describeBand(band: DifficultyBand): string {\\r\\n  return band.min === band.max\\r\\n    ? `difficulty ${band.min}`\\r\\n    : `difficulty ${band.min}-${band.max}`;\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': ## assistant

Pre-execution review:

1. **Repeated process startup is the main verified overhead.** Every `bash system/scripts/lsatu assign` fetches `origin/main`, checks the worktree, generates the entry file, launches `vite-node`, authenticates, and fetches the student list. Daily commands plus dry runs repeat all of this. Add a file-based batch path that syncs once, authenticates once, resolves the student once, preflights all entries, then calls the app’s existing `assignWork()` serially.

2. **Existing guided drills should be the first selection path.** The previous run authored three new MBT drill records. This request specifically asks for corresponding existing conditionals drills. Read the live catalog once; map IDs/slugs, titles, instruction summaries, content mode, filters, native difficulty bounds, question counts, and actual eligible pool difficulty counts. Store the generated catalog outside always-loaded skill text, with source timestamp and refresh command. Verify selected entries live before writing.

3. **Level labels cannot enforce the requested caps.** `src/lib/studentLevel.ts` maps beginner to 1–3 and intermediate to 2–4. An intermediate override could exceed Joshua’s ceiling of 3. Select native drills with matching bands and check actual eligibility. Explicit `levelOverrides: { [studentId]: null }` preserves the drill’s own range instead of introducing the profile band. Inspect `guidedDrills.ts` and `guidedDrillLevel.ts` for the exact pool-resolution semantics.

4. **The local MCP already exists and is callable.** Current session exposes `mcp__lsat_u_lessons__list_student_classroom_items` and `publish_interactive_lesson`. Extend `system/scripts/lsatu-lesson-mcp.mjs` with:
   - `search_guided_drills`, read-only, compact results.
   - `assign_guided_drill_batch`, structured dates and drill IDs, dry-run option.
   
   Registration is already in `C:/Users/Joshu/.codex/config.toml:160`, invoking `C:\Program Files\nodejs\node.exe` with the local server path. SDK and Zod successfully resolve from sibling LSAT U `node_modules`. Newly exposed tools may require a fresh session; a direct Node/CLI fallback makes them usable immediately.

5. **Batch safety and evidence should be mechanical.** Preflight all dates, exact student identity, access, catalog records, difficulty compatibility, and duplicates before the first assignment. Use `(student_id, guided_drill_id, scheduled_for)` for a duplicate check; return verified existing rows on a rerun without notifying again. Sequential writes preserve clear partial-failure outcomes. Do not automatically retry an uncertain write or a failed notification channel.

6. **Retain the app’s notification behavior.** `assignWork()` defaults to one inbox/bell/email event per assignment; channel failures are successful assignments with partial notification. Report them without reassigning. `assignWorkBundle()` can combine same-day items into one notification, but it does not expose per-item due overrides, so it is not the simplest path for this three-date request.

7. **Generalize verification beyond the authored MBT script.** `verify-guided-spec.mjs` is currently bound to MBT specs and extracts the scheduled date from the slug. Existing native conditional drills need verifier inputs of `{drill_id, scheduled_for, due_date, expected_count, expected_band}`. Read assignment, Classroom, access, and bell state with shared authentication; fetch each deployed section file once rather than once per question.

Skill improvements should be a short ordered recipe: catalog search → live preflight → batch dry run → execute → compact verification → append evidence. Move implementation details behind a reference pointer. The previous run’s notification acceptance and headless verification are documented; independent email receipt and student runner launch remain unverified.

<guided tool> --file <arguments.json> | --json <single-line JSON>\');\\r\\nconst args = JSON.parse(flag === \'--file\' ? fs.readFileSync(path.resolve(value), \'utf8\') : value);\\r\\nconst transport = \'ai-os-guided-operator\', version: \'1.0.0\' });\\r\\ntry {\\r\\n  await client.connect(transport);\\r\\n  const result = await client.callTool({ name, arguments: args }, undefined, { timeout: 180000 });\\r\\n  if (result.isError) process.exitCode = 1;\\r\\n  console.log(JSON.stringify(result.structuredContent ?? result));\\r\\n} finally {\\r\\n  await client.close();\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}}'}, {'type': Content operations live in AI OS, so changing this\\r\\n// server never requires an LSAT U app deployment.\\r\\nimport { projectPath } from \'./project-paths.mjs\';\\r\\nimport { createRequire } from \'node:module\';\\r\\nimport { pathToFileURL, fileURLToPath } from \'node:url\';\\r\\nimport { execFile } from \'node:child_process\';\\r\\nimport { promisify } from \'node:util\';\\r\\nimport path from \'node:path\';\\r\\nimport fs from \'node:fs\';\\r\\n\\r\\nconst vault = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), \'../..\');\\r\\nconst appRequire = createRequire(projectPath(\'lsatu\', \'package.json\'));\\r\\nconst [{ McpServer }, { StdioServerTransport }, { z }] = await Promise.all([\\r\\n  import(pathToFileURL(appRequire.resolve(\'@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/mcp.js\')).href),\\r\\n  import(pathToFileURL(appRequire.resolve(\'@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/stdio.js\')).href),\\r\\n  import(pathToFileURL(appRequire.resolve(\'zod\')).href),\\r\\n]);\\r\\nconst run = promisify(execFile);\\r\\nconst admin = path.join(vault, \'system\', \'scripts\', \'lsatu-admin.mjs\');\\r\\nconst server = new McpServer({ name: \'lsat-u-lessons\', version: \'1.2.0\' });\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function command(args) {\\r\\n  try {\\r\\n    const { stdout } = await run(process.execPath, [admin, ...args], {\\r\\n      cwd: vault, windowsHide: true, maxBuffer: 2 * 1024 * 1024, timeout: 120000,\\r\\n    });\\r\\n    return { content: [{ type: \'text\', text: stdout.trim() }] };\\r\\n  } catch (error) {\\r\\n    return { isError: true, content: [{ type: \'text\', text: String(error.stderr || error.message).trim() }] };\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst guidedPlan = path.join(vault, \'system/scripts/lsatu-guided-plan.mjs\');\\r\\n// Serialize guided writes within this MCP process. No automatic write retries.\\r\\nlet guidedQueue = Promise.resolve();\\r\\nasync function guidedCommand(plan, dryRun) {\\r\\n  const operation = guidedQueue.then(async () => {\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      const { stdout } = await run(process.execPath, [guidedPlan, \'--json\', JSON.stringify(plan), ...(dryRun ? [\'--dry-run\'] : [])], {\\r\\n        cwd: vault, windowsHide: true, maxBuffer: 4 * 1024 * 1024, timeout: 180000,\\r\\n      });\\r\\n      const result = JSON.parse(stdout.trim());\\r\\n      return { content: [{ type: \'text\', text: JSON.stringify(result) }], structuredContent: result };\\r\\n    } catch (error) {\\r\\n      return { isError: true, content: [{ type: \'text\', text: String(error.stderr || error.message).trim() }] };\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  });\\r\\n  guidedQueue = operation.then(() => undefined, () => undefined);\\r\\n  return operation;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst guidedAssignment = z.object({\\r\\n  drill: z.string().min(2).describe(\'Exact published guided drill slug from search_guided_drills.\'),\\r\\n  schedule: z.string().regex(/^\\\\d{4}-\\\\d{2}-\\\\d{2}$/),\\r\\n  due: z.string().regex(/^\\\\d{4}-\\\\d{2}-\\\\d{2}$/),\\r\\n  level: z.enum([\'beginner\', \'intermediate\', \'advanced\']).nullable().optional(),\\r\\n  count: z.number().int().min(1).max(50).optional().describe(\'Omit to retain the existing drill size and matching instruction card.\'),\\r\\n  max_difficulty: z.number().int().min(1).max(5).optional().describe(\'Hard preflight ceiling on every question that can be drawn at the requested level.\'),\\r\\n});\\r\\nconst guidedSchema = {\\r\\n  id: z.string().optional(),\\r\\n  student: z.string().min(2).describe(\'One exact student name or email.\'),\\r\\n  assignments: z.array(guidedAssignment).min(1).max(14),\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\nserver.registerTool(\'search_guided_drills\', {\\r\\n  title: \'Find matching LSAT U guided drills\',\\r\\n  description: \'Search the mapped guided drill catalog by title, question type, reasoning, feature, or slug. Returns compact difficulty/pool metadata and snapshot time; assignment preflight rechecks live data.\',\\r\\n  inputSchema: { query: z.string().default(\'\'), max_difficulty: z.number().int().min(1).max(5).optional(), limit: z.number().int().min(1).max(30).default(12) },\\r\\n  annotations: { readOnlyHint: true },\\r\\n}, ({ query, max_difficulty, limit }) => {\\r\\n  const catalog = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(vault, \'references/lsatu-guided-drill-catalog.json\'), \'utf8\'));\\r\\n  const words = query.toLowerCase().trim().split(/\\\\s+/).filter(Boolean);\\r\\n  const matching = catalog.drills.filter(d => d.is_published && words.every(word => JSON.stringify([d.slug, d.title, d.bank_filter, d.reasoning_types]).toLowerCase().includes(word)) && (max_difficulty == null || (d.pool_summary.difficulty_max != null && d.pool_summary.difficulty_max <= max_difficulty)));\\r\\n  const result = { snapshot: catalog.meta.generated_at, total_matches: matching.length, drills: matching.slice(0, limit).map(d => ({ slug: d.slug, title: d.title, count: d.question_count, minutes: d.estimated_minutes, catalog_level: d.level, native_band: [d.bank_filter.difficulty_min, d.bank_filter.difficulty_max], pool: d.pool_summary, bank_filter: d.bank_filter })) };\\r\\n  return { content: [{ type: \'text\', text: JSON.stringify(result) }], structuredContent: result };\\r\\n});\\r\\n\\r\\nserver.registerTool(\'preview_guided_drill_batch\', {\\r\\n  title: \'Preflight a guided drill schedule\',\\r\\n  description: \'Read-only live preflight for one student and up to 14 guided assignments. Resolves identity/access, published drills, deployed question pools, difficulty ceilings, dates, and existing assignments. One sync and login for the whole plan.\',\\r\\n  inputSchema: guidedSchema,\\r\\n  annotations: { readOnlyHint: true },\\r\\n}, plan => guidedCommand(plan, true));\\r\\n\\r\\nserver.registerTool(\'assign_guided_drill_batch\', {\\r\\n  title: \'Assign and verify a guided drill schedule\',\\r\\n  description: \'Assign existing guided drills to one named student through the app assignWork. Preflights every item before writes, verifies saved assignments and Classroom dates/visibility, and reports inbox/bell/email results. A repeat returns matching existing assignments without notifying again. No browser or app deployment.\',\\r\\n  inputSchema: guidedSchema,\\r\\n  annotations: { readOnlyHint: false, destructiveHint: false, idempotentHint: true },\\r\\n}, plan => guidedCommand(plan, false));\\r\\n\\r\\nserver.registerTool(\'publish_interactive_lesson\', {\\r\\n  title: \'Assign an interactive LSAT U lesson\',\\r\\n  description: \'Assign a complete HTML lesson directly to one student. Verifies byte-for-byte content, visibility, and one active copy; replaces only prior versions of the same lesson. No browser or Lovable publish.\',\\r\\n  inputSchema: {\\r\\n    student: z.string().min(2).describe(\'Exact student email or unambiguous name.\'),\\r\\n    html_file: z.string().min(1).describe(\'Absolute path to marked, self-contained HTML within AI OS.\'),\\r\\n    title: z.string().min(1).max(160),\\r\\n    dry_run: z.boolean().optional(),\\r\\n  },\\r\\n  annotations: { readOnlyHint: false, destructiveHint: true, idempotentHint: true },\\r\\n}, ({ student, html_file, title, dry_run }) => command([\\r\\n  \'post-lesson\', student, html_file, \'--title\', title, ...(dry_run ? [\'--dry-run\'] : []),\\r\\n]));\\r\\n\\r\\nserver.registerTool(\'list_student_classroom_items\', {\\r\\n  title: \'List a student’s LSAT U Classroom items\',\\r\\n  description: \'Read the current Classroom items and IDs for one student.\',\\r\\n  inputSchema: { student: z.string().min(2) },\\r\\n  annotations: { readOnlyHint: true },\\r\\n}, ({ student }) => command([\'items\', student]));\\r\\n\\r\\nawait server.connect(new StdioServerTransport());\\r\\n"}}'}]

Math.round((total / measured) * 10) / 10 : null,\\r\\n    trapMatches,\\r\\n  };\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function resolveLeveledDrillQids(input: LeveledDrillInput): LeveledDrillResolution {\\r\\n  const {\\r\\n    pool,\\r\\n    questionCount,\\r\\n    drillQids,\\r\\n    assignmentQids,\\r\\n    knownQids,\\r\\n    level,\\r\\n    rng = Math.random,\\r\\n    stats = null,\\r\\n    empiricalWeight = 0,\\r\\n    targetTrapTags = null,\\r\\n    trapWeight = 0,\\r\\n  } = input;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const band = level ? levelBand(level) : null;\\r\\n\\r\\n  // 1. An exact list always wins, in full and in authored order.\\r\\n  //\\r\\n  // It is deliberately NOT filtered by the band. Filtering a pinned list is the\\r\\n  // only way this function could return a two-question drill, and a silently\\r\\n  // short drill is worse than an off-level one. If a pinned set needs a level\\r\\n  // variant, that is two drills, not a subset of one.\\r\\n  //\\r\\n  // (No seeded drill pins a list today: apply-guided-drill-taxonomy.mjs strips\\r\\n  // question_qids on purpose, because drills are re-attemptable pools. This\\r\\n  // branch exists for hand-pinned assignments.)\\r\\n  const pinned = cleanQids(assignmentQids ?? drillQids ?? [], knownQids);\\r\\n  if (pinned.length > 0) {\\r\\n    const anyOutOfBand =\\r\\n      band != null &&\\r\\n      pool.some((q) => pinned.includes(q.qid) && !isInBand(q.difficulty, band));\\r\\n    return {\\r\\n      qids: pinned,\\r\\n      source: \'pinned\',\\r\\n      level,\\r\\n      band,\\r\\n      poolCount: pool.length,\\r\\n      inBandCount: band ? pool.filter((q) => isInBand(q.difficulty, band)).length : pool.length,\\r\\n      outOfBandUsed: 0,\\r\\n      warning: band && anyOutOfBand ? \'pinned_level_not_applied\' : null,\\r\\n      ...describeSet(pinned, stats, targetTrapTags),\\r\\n    };\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  // 2. Draw from the pool. `want` reproduces the existing runtime rule.\\r\\n  const want = questionCount ?? Math.min(pool.length, 10);\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (pool.length === 0) {\\r\\n    return {\\r\\n      qids: [],\\r\\n      source: \'pool\',\\r\\n      level,\\r\\n      band,\\r\\n      poolCount: 0,\\r\\n      inBandCount: 0,\\r\\n      outOfBandUsed: 0,\\r\\n      warning: \'empty\',\\r\\n      statsCoverage: null,\\r\\n      meanCorrectPct: null,\\r\\n      trapMatches: 0,\\r\\n    };\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  // No level: byte-identical to the behaviour before levels existed.\\r\\n  if (!band) {\\r\\n    const qids = shuffle(pool, rng).slice(0, want).map((q) => q.qid);\\r\\n    return {\\r\\n      qids,\\r\\n      source: \'pool\',\\r\\n      level: null,\\r\\n      band: null,\\r\\n      poolCount: pool.length,\\r\\n      inBandCount: pool.length,\\r\\n      outOfBandUsed: 0,\\r\\n      warning: null,\\r\\n      ...describeSet(qids, stats, targetTrapTags),\\r\\n    };\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Rank, do not filter. The band decides preference order, never eligibility,\\r\\n  // so the drill is always the length it was meant to be:\\r\\n  //   qids.length === min(want, pool.length), at every level.\\r\\n  // An empty or short drill is impossible by construction rather than by\\r\\n  // fallback, which matters because a two-question drill reads as a bug to the\\r\\n  // student and there is no way for them to tell it was a levelling decision.\\r\\n  //\\r\\n  // Two optional signals ride on top, and each is confined to one job:\\r\\n  //   empiricalWeight decides MEMBERSHIP  (is this question really at level?)\\r\\n  //   trapWeight      decides ORDER       (which of the eligible ones first?)\\r\\n  // Keeping them apart is what makes each provable on its own, and what lets\\r\\n  // both default to off without a special case threaded through the algorithm.\\r\\n  //\\r\\n  const useEmpirical = empiricalWeight > 0 && stats != null && stats.size > 0;\\r\\n\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * The difficulty this question is treated as having. With no blending this\\r\\n   * is the bank\'s label, so the comparison below stays the same `isInBand`\\r\\n   * call the module has always made.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  const gradeOf = (q: LRQuestion) =>\\r\\n    useEmpirical ? effectiveDifficulty(q.difficulty, stats.get(q.qid), empiricalWeight) : q.difficulty;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const inBand: LRQuestion[] = [];\\r\\n  const outOfBand: LRQuestion[] = [];\\r\\n  for (const q of pool) {\\r\\n    const grade = gradeOf(q);\\r\\n    const eligible = useEmpirical ? isInBandFractional(grade, band) : isInBand(q.difficulty, band);\\r\\n    (eligible ? inBand : outOfBand).push(q);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Order the eligible group. `trapWeight` literally trades freshness for aim:\\r\\n  // at 0 the key is pure rng and this is a shuffle, at 1 it is pure targeting\\r\\n  // and a re-attempt returns the same questions in the same order. The jitter\\r\\n  // is not decoration — the re-attempt-fresh model depends on it, so the dial\\r\\n  // never silently reaches 1.\\r\\n  const rankByTrap = trapWeight > 0 && stats != null && (targetTrapTags?.length ?? 0) > 0;\\r\\n  const ordered = rankByTrap\\r\\n    ? inBand\\r\\n        .map((q) => ({\\r\\n          q,\\r\\n          key: trapWeight * trapScore(stats.get(q.qid), targetTrapTags) + (1 - trapWeight) * rng(),\\r\\n        }))\\r\\n        .sort((a, b) => b.key - a.key)\\r\\n        .map((entry) => entry.q)\\r\\n    : shuffle(inBand, rng);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const picked = ordered.slice(0, want);\\r\\n\\r\\n  let outOfBandUsed = 0;\\r\\n  if (picked.length < want) {\\r\\n    // Top up with the nearest-miss questions first, so a beginner short on\\r\\n    // level-3s gets level-4s before level-5s. Mirrors resolveGuidedDrillPool in src/lib/guidedDrills.ts.\\r\\n\\r\\nimport fs from \'node:fs\';\\r\\nimport path from \'node:path\';\\r\\n\\r\\nconst BANK_DIR = path.resolve(\'public/data\');\\r\\nconst STATS = path.resolve(\'public/data/question-stats.json\');\\r\\nconst SEED = path.resolve(\'data/guided-drills/_seed.json\');\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Every LR question the local bank files know, keyed by qid. */\\r\\nexport function loadBank() {\\r\\n  const byQid = new Map();\\r\\n  for (const file of fs.readdirSync(BANK_DIR).filter((n) => /-LR\\\\.json$/.test(n))) {\\r\\n    let arr;\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      arr = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(BANK_DIR, file), \'utf8\'));\\r\\n    } catch {\\r\\n      continue;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    if (!Array.isArray(arr)) continue;\\r\\n    for (const q of arr) {\\r\\n      byQid.set(`${q.practiceTest}-S${q.sectionNumber}-Q${q.questionNumber}`, q);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return byQid;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function loadSeed() {\\r\\n  return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(SEED, \'utf8\'));\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Cohort stats hydrated into the shape resolveLeveledDrillQids expects.\\r\\n * The sidecar\'s keys are abbreviated; this mirrors hydrate() in\\r\\n * src/lib/questionStats.ts.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function loadStats() {\\r\\n  if (!fs.existsSync(STATS)) return new Map();\\r\\n  const payload = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(STATS, \'utf8\'));\\r\\n  const map = new Map();\\r\\n  for (const [qid, raw] of Object.entries(payload.q ?? {})) {\\r\\n    map.set(qid, {\\r\\n      correctChoice: raw.c,\\r\\n      correctPct: raw.cp,\\r\\n      empiricalDifficulty: raw.ed,\\r\\n      topTrapLetter: raw.tl,\\r\\n      topTrapTag: raw.tt,\\r\\n      topTrapTagRaw: raw.ttr,\\r\\n      topTrapPct: raw.tp,\\r\\n      trapSpread: raw.ts,\\r\\n      choicePcts: raw.p,\\r\\n    });\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return map;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Mirrors resolveGuidedDrillPool in src/lib/guidedDrills.ts. */\\r\\nexport function resolvePool(bankFilter, bank) {\\r\\n  const f = bankFilter ?? {};\\r\\n  const qtypes = new Set((f.qtypes || []).map((s) => s.toLowerCase()));\\r\\n  const features = new Set((f.features || []).map((s) => s.toLowerCase()));\\r\\n  const reasoning = new Set((f.reasoning_types || []).map((s) => s.toLowerCase()));\\r\\n  const dmin = f.difficulty_min ?? null;\\r\\n  const dmax = f.difficulty_max ?? null;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const out = [];\\r\\n  for (const [qid, q] of bank) {\\r\\n    if (qtypes.size && !qtypes.has((q.questionType || \'\').toLowerCase())) continue;\\r\\n    if (features.size && !(q.questionFeatures || []).some((x) => features.has((x || \'\').toLowerCase())))\\r\\n      continue;\\r\\n    if (reasoning.size && !(q.reasoningType || []).some((x) => reasoning.has((x || \'\').toLowerCase())))\\r\\n      continue;\\r\\n    const d = q.questionDifficulty;\\r\\n    if (!Number.isFinite(d)) continue;\\r\\n    if (dmin != null && d < dmin) continue;\\r\\n    if (dmax != null && d > dmax) continue;\\r\\n    out.push({ qid, difficulty: d, type: q.questionType });\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return out;\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]
